- **lxd-docker-firewall-conflict**: Diagnose and fix the well-known Docker/LXD firewall conflict on a host running both. Docker sets the iptables FORWARD chain policy to DROP and accepts only its own bridges, so forwarded traffic from the LXD bridge (lxdbr0) is silently dropped and LXD containers/VMs get no outbound internet (the host itself is fine). Fix: accept the LXD bridge in the DOCKER-USER chain, then persist it with a systemd unit ordered after docker.service. - **lythoskill-reminders**: Reconciles a declarative session-reminder registry (cortex/REMINDERS.toml) against the host's session-scoped cron tools. On entry: diff the registry against CronList, CronCreate the missing (cron + prompt copied verbatim), CronDelete orphans and re-calibrated entries. Multi-session safety via a lease lock with heartbeat; prompts are zero-context executable (task + file pointers + Done criteria + anti-redo notes). Decisions: ADR-20261013143648588.
